country_event = {
	id = 1472073
	title = "0"
	desc = "0"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = SOV
		AI = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			OR = {
				tag = RUS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "0"
		set_country_flag = not_ai
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				AI = no
				war = no
			}
			country_event = { id=1472075 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	title = "CHANGE_YOUR_WORK"
	desc = "CHANGE_YOUR_WORK_desk"
	picture = "Celebration"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = "CHANGE_YOUR_WORK_ok"
		clr_country_flag = not_ai
		change_tag_no_core_switch = NGF
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = 1472259
	title = "0"
	desc = "0"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = OLD
		AI = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			OR = {
				tag = RUS
				tag = SOV
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "0"
		set_country_flag = not_ai
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				AI = no
				war = no
			}
			country_event = { id=1472261 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	id = 1472260

	trigger = {
